6//! NIST ACVP ML-DSA (FIPS 204) vectors: deterministic key generation
7//! (Algorithm 6, `seed` → `pk`/`sk`), HashML-DSA signature generation
8//! (external interface, `preHash = preHash`) and HashML-DSA signature
9//! verification.
10//!
11//! ## Provenance
12//!
13//! Extracted verbatim (hex strings copied unmodified) from the NIST
14//! ACVP-Server repository, `gen-val/json-files/<dir>/internalProjection.json`,
15//! at commit `2972def23bf9f3680c2c531561ed9bdd0f1086ad`.
16//!
17//! Repository: <https://github.com/usnistgov/ACVP-Server>, path
18//! `gen-val/json-files/<dir>/internalProjection.json`, where `<dir>` and the
19//! SHA-256 of each fetched file are:
20//!
21//! - `ML-DSA-keyGen-FIPS204`:
22//!   `e67ee6540d40e11506c3c4e3b1f79fc1cefcd49820db99fc61f87cc8ba463baf`
23//! - `ML-DSA-sigGen-FIPS204`:
24//!   `72dcaf5f69853ca267ccd16af9cb40949786aca0fcfbf05d1ebeba132b93af22`
25//! - `ML-DSA-sigVer-FIPS204`:
26//!   `a41362abd3391b4858cf5d04556b6662881b0ac90eebf886c5c7271b0baf69d5`
27//!
28//! `vsId` 42 in all three files. The `HASH_SIGGEN_VECTORS` were chosen from
29//! the `external` / `preHash` groups so that every case has a non-empty
30//! context (the LatticeArc API requires one) and uses one of the pre-hash
31//! functions `PrehashAlgorithm` supports at that parameter set per the
32//! FIPS 204 §5.4 strength floor (SHA2-512 / SHAKE-256 everywhere; SHA2-256 /
33//! SHAKE-128 at ML-DSA-44 only). `deterministic = true` groups fix
34//! `rnd = 0^32`; the three `deterministic = false` cases carry their `rnd`.
35//! `HASH_SIGVER_VECTORS` include one valid case and five NIST-mutated cases
36//! (modified commitment / hint / message) that must be rejected.
37//!
38//! Fetched 2026-10-20. Do not edit by hand; regenerate from the source
39//! files if NIST republishes them, and update `CHECKSUMS.sha256`.
40
41/// FIPS 204 Algorithm 6 key-generation vector (`ML-DSA-keyGen-FIPS204`).
42pub struct MlDsaKeyGenVector {
43    pub tc_id: u32,
44    pub parameter_set: &'static str,
45    /// 32-byte seed `ξ` (hex).
46    pub seed: &'static str,
47    pub pk: &'static str,
48    pub sk: &'static str,
49}
50
51/// HashML-DSA signature-generation vector (`ML-DSA-sigGen-FIPS204`,
52/// `signatureInterface = external`, `preHash = preHash`).
53pub struct HashMlDsaSigGenVector {
54    pub tc_id: u32,
55    pub parameter_set: &'static str,
56    /// ACVP `hashAlg` name (`SHA2-256`, `SHA2-512`, `SHAKE-128`, `SHAKE-256`).
57    pub hash_alg: &'static str,
58    /// Hedging value `rnd` (hex, 32 bytes); all-zero for the deterministic variant.
59    pub rnd: &'static str,
60    pub context: &'static str,
61    pub message: &'static str,
62    pub pk: &'static str,
63    pub sk: &'static str,
64    pub signature: &'static str,
65}
66
67/// HashML-DSA signature-verification vector (`ML-DSA-sigVer-FIPS204`,
68/// `signatureInterface = external`, `preHash = preHash`).
69pub struct HashMlDsaSigVerVector {
70    pub tc_id: u32,
71    pub parameter_set: &'static str,
72    pub hash_alg: &'static str,
73    pub context: &'static str,
74    pub message: &'static str,
75    pub pk: &'static str,
76    pub signature: &'static str,
77    pub test_passed: bool,
78    /// NIST's stated reason for the expected outcome.
79    pub reason: &'static str,
80}
